2. 死亡族:玩家死亡音、敌怪死亡音(NPC_Killed 系列按死亡方式)
3. 战斗动作:挥武器/射击(各武器类音)、喝药、吃食物、扔手雷
4. 交互族:开门/开箱/放块/挖块(各材质音分档:石/土/草?)、切物品栏、拾取物品(coin/普通)

6. 环境族:雷声(已审近似)、雨声/风声有无、BGM 切换交叉淡入
7. UI 族:按钮/重铸(普通 tink vs 最优档专属)/成就解锁/宠物召唤
8. 播放条件:同一音效重复触发的冷却、Boss 出场 Roar 只首播、x=-1 不衰减(UI 声)

90	export class Sfx {
91	  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
92	  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
93	  /** 在途解码中的文件（防重复 fetch） */
94	  private pending = new Set<string>();
95	  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */
96	  private failed = new Set<string>();
97	  muted = false;
98	  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
99	  master = 1;
100	
101	  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
102	  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
103	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
104	    if (buf) return buf;
105	    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
106	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
107	    if (!ctx) return null;
108	    this.pending.add(file);
109	    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
110	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
111	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
112	      .then((decoded) => {
113	        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
114	        else this.failed.add(file);
115	      })
116	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
117	      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
118	    return null;
119	  }
120	
121	  /** 定向预热（不阻塞）：把指定 wav 后台取齐，播放时即命中 */
122	  preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>) {
123	    for (const f of files) this.ensureBuffer(f);
124	  }
125	
126	  /** 按逻辑名预热（WAV_MAP 展开到具体文件） */
127	  preloadNames(names: SfxName[]) {
128	    for (const n of names) for (const f of WAV_MAP[n] ?? []) this.ensureBuffer(f);
129	  }
130	
131	  // ---- 雨声层：原版 RainAmbience（Music_28.mp3，XACT Wave Bank 同源提取）循环 ----
132	  private rainGain: GainNode | null = null;
133	  private rainSrc: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
134	  private rainLoaded = false;
135	  /** 设置雨声强度 0-1（每帧调用；0 时静音不拆节点——重启有冷启动开销） */
136	  setRain(vol: number) {
137	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
138	    if (!ctx) return;
139	    if (!this.rainGain) {
140	      const g = ctx.createGain();
141	      g.gain.value = 0;
142	      g.connect(ctx.destination);
143	      this.rainGain = g;
144	      // 原版雨环境音轨（RainAmbience）懒加载循环；加载失败静默（雨声可选）
145	      fetch('audios/music/Music_28.mp3')
146	        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
147	        .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
148	        .then((buf) => {
149	          if (!buf) return;
150	          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
151	          src.buffer = buf;
152	          src.loop = true;
153	          src.connect(this.rainGain!);
154	          src.start();
155	          this.rainSrc = src;
156	          this.rainLoaded = true;
157	        })
158	        .catch(() => {});
159	    }
160	    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)) * this.master * 0.6;
161	    this.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(v, ctx.currentTime, 0.4);
162	  }
163	
164	  /** 世界声距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:160 SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500px） */
165	  static readonly ATTEN = 2500;
166	  /** 监听器（原版 Main.Camera.Center，相机中心非玩家）；Game 每帧更新。未进世界=(0,0) */
167	  private lx = 0; private ly = 0;
168	  setListener(x: number, y: number) { this.lx = x; this.ly = y; }
169	  /** 原版距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:381-401）：dist ≥2500 → 不播；
170	   *  vol × (1 - dist/2500)。不传坐标（x=-1 语义）→ 全量（UI/玩家自身动作声） */
171	  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {
172	    if (x == null || y == null) return vol;
173	    const d = Math.hypot(x - this.lx, y - this.ly);
174	    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
175	    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
176	  }
177	
178	  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
179	    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
180	    if (!this.ctx) {
181	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
182	      if (!AC) return null;
183	      this.ctx = new AC();
184	    }
185	    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
186	    return this.ctx;
187	  }
188	
189	  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
190	  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
191	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
192	    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
193	    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol, x, y);
194	  }
195	
196	  /** 播放任意 wav 文件列表（随机选 + 音高扰动）；供怪物专属声效用 */
197	  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
198	    if (!files.length) return false;
199	    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);
200	  }
201	
202	  /** 按文件名直播（缓冲未就位时触发后台加载，本播返回 false 走合成兜底） */
203	  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
204	    const ctx = this.ensure();
205	    if (!ctx) return false;
206	    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
207	    if (v <= 0) return false;
208	    vol = v;
209	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
210	    if (!buf) return false;
211	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
212	    src.buffer = buf;
213	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
214	    const g = ctx.createGain();
215	    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
216	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
217	    src.start();
218	    return true;
219	  }
220	
221	  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
222	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。
223	    // 带坐标时衰减（atten），≤0 直接不播——合成回退也不触发
224	    if (x != null && y != null && this.atten(vol, x, y) <= 0) return;
225	    if (this.playWav(name, vol, x, y)) return;
226	    const ctx = this.ensure();
227	    if (!ctx) return;
228	    const t = ctx.currentTime;
229	    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
230	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
231	      const g = ctx.createGain();
232	      o.type = type;
233	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
234	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
235	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
236	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
237	      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
238	      o.start(t + delay);
239	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
240	    };
241	    switch (name) {
242	      case 'hit': osc('square', 320, 110, 0.09, 0.12); break;
243	      case 'chop': osc('triangle', 220, 80, 0.12, 0.16); break;
244	      case 'hurt': osc('sawtooth', 180, 60, 0.22, 0.14); break;
245	      case 'killed': osc('square', 260, 60, 0.25, 0.14); break;
246	      case 'pkilled': osc('sawtooth', 160, 40, 0.6, 0.18); break;
247	      case 'zombie': osc('sawtooth', 90, 55, 0.5, 0.1); break;
248	      case 'roar': osc('sawtooth', 70, 40, 0.9, 0.2); break;
249	      case 'pickup': osc('sine', 520, 990, 0.09, 0.1); osc('sine', 780, 1320, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05); break;
250	      case 'dig': osc('square', 140, 90, 0.05, 0.06); break;
251	      case 'tink': osc('sine', 900, 500, 0.06, 0.08); break;
252	      case 'place': osc('triangle', 260, 120, 0.08, 0.12); break;
253	      case 'drink': osc('sine', 300, 620, 0.06, 0.08); osc('sine', 420, 780, 0.06, 0.07, 0.07); osc('sine', 560, 940, 0.06, 0.06, 0.14); break;
254	      case 'splash': osc('sine', 700, 200, 0.18, 0.1); break;
255	      case 'summon': {
256	        // 召唤杖 UseSound(Item_44)近似:短促"噗"——高频下扫正弦 + 一撮噪声气声
257	        osc('sine', 900, 260, 0.16, 0.14);
258	        osc('triangle', 1400, 500, 0.08, 0.06);
259	        break;
260	      }
261	      case 'whipCrack': {
262	        // 鞭响(Item_152)近似:高频噪声急促炸开 + 快速衰减
263	        const wDur = 0.12, wn = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * wDur);
264	        const wBuf = ctx.createBuffer(1, wn, ctx.sampleRate);
265	        const wd = wBuf.getChannelData(0);
266	        for (let i = 0; i < wn; i++) wd[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / wn, 3);
267	        const wSrc = ctx.createBufferSource();
268	        wSrc.buffer = wBuf;
269	        const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
270	        hp.type = 'highpass';
271	        hp.frequency.value = 1200;
272	        const wg = ctx.createGain();
273	        wg.gain.value = 0.18 * this.master;
274	        wSrc.connect(hp).connect(wg).connect(ctx.destination);
275	        wSrc.start(t);
276	        break;
277	      }
278	      case 'explosion': {
279	        // 噪声爆发近似(原版 Item_14):一次性白噪 buffer + 低频 thump。
280	        // ★曾无此分支:按需加载首播(wav 还在 fetch)完全静音 = "炸弹没有音效"主因
281	        const dur = 0.5, n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur);
282	        const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate);
283	        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
284	        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / n, 2.2);
285	        const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
286	        src.buffer = buf;
287	        const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
288	        lp.type = 'lowpass';
289	        lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(900, t);
290	        lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + dur);
291	        const g = ctx.createGain();
292	        g.gain.value = 0.5 * this.master;
293	        src.connect(lp).connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
294	        src.start(t);
295	        osc('sine', 90, 35, 0.35, 0.35); // 低频冲击
296	        break;
297	      }
298	      case 'coin': osc('sine', 990, 1400, 0.07, 0.08); osc('sine', 1320, 1760, 0.09, 0.06, 0.05); break;
299	      case 'door_open': osc('triangle', 180, 320, 0.12, 0.1); break;
300	      case 'door_close': osc('triangle', 320, 160, 0.1, 0.1); break;
301	      case 'shatter': osc('square', 1400, 300, 0.15, 0.1); break;
302	      case 'drown': osc('sine', 400, 90, 0.4, 0.1); break;
303	      case 'mirror': osc('sine', 700, 1900, 0.3, 0.06); osc('sine', 1400, 2800, 0.25, 0.04, 0.1); break;   // 魔镜闪烁上行
304	      case 'conch': osc('sine', 220, 330, 0.25, 0.08); osc('sine', 330, 440, 0.3, 0.06, 0.2); break;        // 海螺低鸣
305	    }
306	  }
307	}
308

1	// 原版 BGM 系统（LegacyAudioSystem.cs 交叉淡化语义 1:1）：
2	// WebAudio AudioBufferSourceNode 无缝循环（loop=true，解码后内存播放，零接缝）——
3	// 修复 <audio loop> 播 mp3 的循环点卡顿 + 切曲冷启动顿挫（原版 XACT 同为解码循环）。
4	// musicFade[id] ±0.005/帧 逼近目标曲（:281 UpdateCommonTrack / :309 TowardStopping）。
5	// audios/music/Music_<id>.mp3（tools/xwb-extract.mjs 提取）。
6	import { MUSIC } from '../data/Music';
7	
8	const TITLE_ID = MUSIC.TitleIntro; // 50
9	
10	interface Track {
11	  src: AudioBufferSourceNode;
12	  gain: GainNode;
13	  fade: number;   // 0..1（≈原版 Main.musicFade[id]）
14	}
15	
16	export class AudioSystem {
17	  private ac: AudioContext | null = null;
18	  private pool = new Map<number, Track>();
19	  private buffers = new Map<number, AudioBuffer>();
20	  private decoding = new Set<number>();
21	  /** 目标曲目（≈原版 Main.curMusic；0 = 静音） */
22	  curMusic = 0;
23	  muted = false;
24	  /** 音乐音量 0..1（options.musicVol，M6 设置页） */
25	  volume = 0.35;
26	  private rafId = 0;
27	  private lastTs = 0;
28	
29	  constructor() {
30	    this.startLoop();
31	  }
32	
33	  private audio(): AudioContext | null {
34	    if (!this.ac) {
35	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
36	      if (!AC) return null;
37	      this.ac = new AC();
38	    }
39	    if (this.ac.state === 'suspended') this.ac.resume().catch(() => { /* 手势前恢复被拒，播放时重试 */ });
40	    return this.ac;
41	  }
42	
43	  /** 解码缓存（每曲一次；解码失败标记 null 防重复请求） */
44	  private async buffer(id: number): Promise<AudioBuffer | null> {
45	    const hit = this.buffers.get(id);
46	    if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
47	    if (this.decoding.has(id)) return null; // 解码中：本帧跳过，就绪后 track() 会补建
48	    this.decoding.add(id);
49	    try {
50	      const r = await fetch(`audios/music/Music_${id}.mp3`);
51	      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(String(r.status));
52	      const buf = await this.ac!.decodeAudioData(await r.arrayBuffer());
53	      this.buffers.set(id, buf);
54	      return buf;
55	    } catch {
56	      this.buffers.set(id, null as unknown as AudioBuffer); // 永久失败标记
57	      return null;
58	    } finally {
59	      this.decoding.delete(id);
60	    }
61	  }
62	
63	  /** 建立播放源（目标曲就绪后调用；重复调用幂等） */
64	  private track(id: number) {
65	    const ac = this.audio();
66	    if (!ac) return;
67	    if (this.pool.has(id)) return;
68	    const buf = this.buffers.get(id);
69	    if (!buf) return; // 未就绪：ensureTrack 的解码回调里补建
70	    const src = ac.createBufferSource();
71	    src.buffer = buf;
72	    src.loop = true;
73	    const gain = ac.createGain();
74	    gain.gain.value = 0;
75	    src.connect(gain).connect(ac.destination);
76	    src.start();
77	    this.pool.set(id, { src, gain, fade: 0 });
78	  }
79	
80	  /** rAF 驱动淡化（原版按游戏帧 ±0.005，这里按 60fps 归一时间步进） */
81	  private startLoop() {
82	    if (this.rafId) return;
83	    const step = (ts: number) => {
84	      const dt = this.lastTs ? Math.min(100, ts - this.lastTs) : 16.7;
85	      this.lastTs = ts;
86	      const frames = dt / (1000 / 60);
87	      this.tickFade(frames * 0.005);
88	      this.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
89	    };
90	    this.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
91	  }
92	
93	  /** 步进淡化量 d（0.005/帧）：目标曲 fade+=d 至 1，其余 -=d 至 0 并停播 */
94	  private tickFade(d: number) {
95	    if (this.muted) {
96	      for (const [id, t] of this.pool) {
97	        if (t.fade > 0) { t.fade = 0; t.gain.gain.value = 0; }
98	      }
99	      return;
100	    }
101	    for (const [id, t] of this.pool) {
102	      const target = id === this.curMusic ? 1 : 0;
103	      if (t.fade < target) {
104	        t.fade = Math.min(target, t.fade + d);
105	        // AudioContext 挂起态（浏览器手势前）：gain 无声但源持续跑，恢复后即出声
106	      } else if (t.fade > target) {
107	        t.fade = Math.max(target, t.fade - d);
108	        if (t.fade === 0) {
109	          t.src.stop();
110	          this.pool.delete(id);
111	          continue;
112	        }
113	      }
114	      t.gain.gain.value = Math.min(1, t.fade) * this.volume;
115	    }
116	  }
117	
118	  setVolume(v: number) {
119	    this.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
120	  }
121	
122	  /** 切换目标曲目（≈原版 curMusic 赋值；实际音量由淡化循环渐变） */
123	  playMusic(id: number) {
124	    if (this.muted) return;
125	    if (this.curMusic === id) return;
126	    this.curMusic = id;
127	    if (id === 0) return;
128	    const ac = this.audio();
129	    if (!ac) return;
130	    // 缓冲未就绪：解码完成后补建源（此时才真正起播，无冷启动顿挫）
131	    if (!this.buffers.has(id)) {
132	      void this.buffer(id).then((buf) => { if (buf && this.curMusic === id) this.track(id); });
133	    } else {
134	      this.track(id);
135	    }
136	  }
137	
138	  /** 兼容旧调用：'title' = 原版标题曲；'main' = 交由环境选曲接管（此处仅解除标题曲） */
139	  play(which: 'title' | 'main') {
140	    if (this.muted) return;
141	    if (which === 'title') this.playMusic(TITLE_ID);
142	    else if (this.curMusic === TITLE_ID) this.playMusic(0);
143	  }
144	
145	  /** 旧昼夜变奏钩子：曲目切换已由选曲链接管（昼/夜曲不同 id），无操作 */
146	  setDayNight(_isDay: boolean) { /* no-op */ }
147	
148	  stop() {
149	    this.curMusic = 0;
150	  }
151	
152	  toggleMute(): boolean {
153	    this.muted = !this.muted;
154	    if (this.muted) this.stop();
155	    return this.muted;
156	  }
157	}
158	

```

    // 落地摔伤结算（幸运马掌免疫；落在水里豁免——水有缓冲）
    if (this.onGround && this.fallStartY !== null) {
      const fallTiles = (this.y - this.fallStartY) / TILE;
      this.fallStartY = null;
      const feetLiq = world.store.liquid[world.store.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor((this.y + this.h - 1) / TILE))];
      const landedInWater = feetLiq > 60 || this.inWater;
      if (landedInWater) { /* 落水缓冲：不计摔伤 */ }
      else if (fallTiles > FALL_SAFE_TILES + this.equipStats.extraFall
        && !(this.hasHorseshoe || this.equipStats.noFallDmg)) {
        const safeT = FALL_SAFE_TILES + this.equipStats.extraFall;
        const t = Math.min(1, (fallTiles - safeT) / (FALL_FATAL_TILES - FALL_SAFE_TILES));
        const dmgPercent = 10 + 90 * t * t * t; // 10%~100% 三次方曲线（Maples 公式）
        const dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round((dmgPercent / 100) * this.hp));
        this.lastDamageCause = { kind: 'fell' };
        this.damage(dmg, this.cx, this.y - 10);
        game.playSfx('hurt');
        game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.y, dmg, false, '#FF5050');
      }
    }

    // 走跑动画计时（仅地面；空中贴图静态不参与动画）
    if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.3 && this.onGround) this.animTime += Math.abs(this.vx);
    else this.animTime = 0;

    // 越界保护
    const maxY = world.h * TILE;
    if (this.y > maxY + 100) this.damage(9999, this.cx, this.y);
    this.prevInputX = this.inputX; // 冲刺双击检测（DoCommonDashHandle release 语义）
    // 血肉墙舌头（Player.WOFTongue :21993-22056，Update 内 myPlayer 段调用）
    this.wofTongue(game, world, dt);

  /** 狂卷之舌 DoT 分块累加器（专家 50 HP/s，:18905-18910 lifeRegen-100；
   *  :19136-19148 -600 阈值 → 每 6t 弹 HurtLifeRegen(5)） */
  private tongueDotAcc = 0;

  /** Player.WOFTongue（Player.cs:21993-22056）1:1：
   *  gross=惊恐 37（见到墙）、tongued=狂卷之舌 38（高于地狱层/绕到墙后）。
   *  - 墙嘴前沿 140px 带且 gross → 每帧 Hurt(50×难度缩放)，强制击退（:22003-22007）
   *  - 未 gross 且身处地狱视野内 → 上 37 + 播 Zombie_10 尖啸（:22009-22012）
   *  - gross 且（高于地狱层 或 绕到墙背后 40px）→ 上 38（:22014-22027）
   *  - tongued：封道具/钩爪（:22032-22041）+ 专家 50 HP/s DoT（:18905-18910）+
   *    舌距中心 >3000px（:22044-22048）或墙抵世界边（:22049-22052）→ KillMe */
  private wofTongue(game: GameHooks, world: import('../world/World').World, dt: number): void {
    if (this.dead) return;
    // :21993-21997 Main.wofNPCIndex<0 / 墙 inactive → 直接返回
    //（enemies 可选调用：测试假宿主常缺该钩子）
    const wall = ((game.enemies?.() ?? []) as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 113) ?? null;
    if (!wall) return;
    // UpdateBuffs :10578-10587：buff 37 在且墙在场 → gross + buffTime 钳 10（apply 续期）；
    // 墙不在场则不续期自然消散（原版 DelBuff）
    const gross = this.buffs.has(BuffType.Horrified);
    if (gross) this.buffs.apply(BuffType.Horrified, 10 / 60);
    const tongued = this.buffs.has(BuffType.TheTongue);
    // :22000-22002 墙嘴前沿 X（墙朝东再退 96px）
    let mouth = wall.x + 40;
    if (wall.facing > 0) mouth -= 96;
    // :22003-22007 墙前 140px 带且 gross → 每帧 50 伤（难度缩放，走墙的难度档）
    if (this.x + this.w > mouth && this.x < mouth + 140 && gross) {
      const dmg = getAttackDamageScaledByDifficulty(50, wall.difficulty);
      this.lastDamageCause = { kind: 'npc', name: 'Wall of Flesh' };
      if (this.damage(dmg, wall.cx, wall.cy)) {
        game.playSfx('hurt');
        game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.y, Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - this.defense * 0.5)), false, '#FF5050');
        // :22005 noKnockback=false——舌击无视钴盾族免击退，沿墙推进方向弹开
        this.vx = (wall.facing >= 0 ? 1 : -1) * 3.4;
        this.vy = -3;
      }
    }

  private suffocateDelay = 0;
  private applyTouchDamage(game: GameHooks, ht: HurtTile): void {
    let blocked = false;
    const dmg = TOUCH_IMMEDIATE.get(ht.type);
    if (dmg !== undefined) {
      this.lastDamageCause = { kind: 'default' }; // PlayerDeathReason.ByOther(3) → 默认包
      if (!this.damage(dmg, this.cx, this.y - 10, false)) {
        blocked = true;
      } else {
        game.playSfx('hurt');
        game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.y, Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - this.defense * 0.5)), false, '#FF5050');
      }
    }
    if (!blocked) {
      if (TOUCH_HOT.has(ht.type)) this.buffs.apply(BuffType.Burning, 20 / 60);   // AddBuff(67,20)
      if (TOUCH_BLEEDING.has(ht.type)) this.buffs.apply(BuffType.Bleeding, (600 + Math.random() * 600) / 60); // AddBuff(30,600-1200)
    }
    if (SUFFOCATE.has(ht.type)) {
      if (this.suffocateDelay < 5) this.suffocateDelay++;       // suffocateDelay<5 递增
      else this.buffs.apply(BuffType.Suffocation, 1 / 60);      // 满后每 tick AddBuff(68,1)

	private static bool AllowShimmerDodge(PlayerDeathReason damageSource, int cooldownCounter, bool dodgeable)
	{
		if (!dodgeable)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (cooldownCounter == 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Entity entity = null;
		if (!damageSource.TryGetCausingEntity(out entity))
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (entity is NPC { active: not false } nPC && (nPC.boss || NPC.GetNPCInvasionGroup(nPC.type) != 0 || NPCID.Sets.CanHitPastShimmer[nPC.type]))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (entity is Projectile { active: not false } projectile && ProjectileID.Sets.CanHitPastShimmer[projectile.type])
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public void HardcoreDeathPenalty()
	{
		if (Main.surviveHardcoreDeath)
		{
			statLifeMax = Math.Max(statLifeMax - 60, 0);
			if (statLifeMax > 0)
			{
				AchievementsHelper.NotifyProgressionEvent(35);
				return;
			}
		}
		KillMeForGood();
	}

	public void KillMeForGood()
	{
		PlayerFileData activePlayerFileData = Main.ActivePlayerFileData;
		if (!activePlayerFileData.ServerSideCharacter)
		{
			bool isCloudSave = activePlayerFileData.IsCloudSave;
			if (FileUtilities.Exists(Main.playerPathName, isCloudSave))
			{
				FileUtilities.Delete(Main.playerPathName, isCloudSave);
			}
			if (FileUtilities.Exists(Main.playerPathName + ".bak", isCloudSave))
			{
				FileUtilities.Delete(Main.playerPathName + ".bak", isCloudSave);
			}
			Main.ActivePlayerFileData = new PlayerFileData();
		}
	}

	private readonly IServiceProvider _services;

	private List<SoundEffectInstance> _trackedInstances;

	public static readonly float SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500f;

	public LegacySoundPlayer(IServiceProvider services)
	{
		_services = services;
		_trackedInstances = new List<SoundEffectInstance>();
		LoadAll();
	}

	public void Reload()
	{
		CreateAllSoundInstances();
	}

	{
		SoundMech[0] = Load("Sounds/Mech_0");

So in vanilla:
- Mining hit (partial, not breaking): `PlaySound(0, x*16, y*16)` = Dig — plays **every hit** while mining (line 45382 in TryHittingWall / and similar in mining tools).
- Placing tile: `PlaySound(0, ...)` = Dig(0) — note: **Dig(0) with style default 1**! Interesting: `PlaySound(0, x, y)` → Style=1 default. Let me check how LegacySoundPlayer handles type 0 style. From the code above, case 0 picks `Main.rand.Next(3)` — random 0-2 regardless of style. So `PlaySound(0,...)` always random dig variant.

So:
- Vanilla place tile → PlaySound(0) = Dig sound
- Implementation 'place' → `place: ['Dig_1', 'Dig_2']` — Dig variants. That's actually equivalent (Dig_0/1/2). ✅ Approximately correct.

- Vanilla mining **each hit** → Dig sound (each swing). Implementation only plays sound **on break** (Game.ts:5308-5315). ❌ Missing per-hit dig sound — that's a notable behavioral difference: vanilla has rhythmic digging sounds per hit.

Let me verify the mining tools function has the per-hit sound. Line 45382 is TryPounding or TryHittingWall. Let me search for the mining one.

	private void ItemCheck_UseMiningTools_ActuallyUseMiningTool(Item sItem, out bool canHitWalls, int x, int y)
	{
		int num = -1;
		int num2 = 0;
		canHitWalls = true;
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (!tile.active())
		{
			return;
		}
		if ((sItem.pick > 0 && !Main.tileAxe[tile.type] && !Main.tileHammer[tile.type]) || (sItem.axe > 0 && Main.tileAxe[tile.type]) || (sItem.hammer > 0 && Main.tileHammer[tile.type]))
		{
			canHitWalls = false;

		num = hitTile.HitObject(x, y, 1);

		if (Main.tileHammer[tile.type])

				num2 += sItem.hammer;

					num2 = 0;

				if (tile.type == 26 && (sItem.hammer < 80 || !Main.hardMode))

					num2 = 0;
					Hurt(PlayerDeathReason.ByOther(4), statLife / 2, -direction);

				AchievementsHelper.CurrentlyMining = true;
				if (hitTile.AddDamage(num, num2) >= 100)

	public void PickTile(int x, int y, int pickPower)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (tile.type == 504)
		{
			return;
		}
		PickTile_DetermineDamage(x, y, pickPower, tile, out var bufferIndex, out var damage);
		if (hitTile.AddDamage(bufferIndex, damage) >= 100)
		{
			IntentionGuesser.AllowTracking();
			AchievementsHelper.CurrentlyMining = true;
			ClearMiningCacheAt(x, y, 1);
			if (Main.netMode == 1 && Main.tileContainer[Main.tile[x, y].type])
			{
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 470 || Main.tile[x, y].type == 475)
				{
					NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, null, 20, x, y);
				}
				else
				{
					WorldGen.KillTile(x, y, fail: true);
					NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, null, 0, x, y, 1f);
				}
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 21)
				{
					NetMessage.SendData(34, -1, -1, null, 1, x, y);
				}
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 467)
				{
					NetMessage.SendData(34, -1, -1, null, 5, x, y);
				}
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 88)
				{
					NetMessage.SendData(34, -1, -1, null, 3, x, y);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				bool flag = Main.tile[x, y].active();
				WorldGen.KillTile(x, y);
				if (!Main.dedServ && flag && !Main.tile[x, y].active())
				{
					AchievementsHelper.HandleMining();
				}
				if (Main.netMode == 1)
				{
					NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, null, 0, x, y);
				}
			}
			AchievementsHelper.CurrentlyMining = false;
		}
		else
		{
			WorldGen.KillTile(x, y, fail: true);
			if (Main.netMode == 1)
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, null, 0, x, y, 1f);
				NetMessage.SendData(125, -1, -1, null, Main.myPlayer, x, y, damage);
			}
		}
		if (damage != 0)
		{
			hitTile.Prune();
		}
	}

	public static void KillTile(int i, int j, bool fail = false, bool effectOnly = false, bool noItem = false)
	{

		{
			return;
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
		if (tile == null)
		{

			Main.tile[i, j] = tile;

		if (!tile.active())

	private void OpenChest(int x, int y, int newChest)
	{
		chest = newChest;
		if (chest != -1 && Main.myPlayer == whoAmI)
		{
			Chest currentContainer = GetCurrentContainer();
			if (currentContainer != null)
			{
				ItemSlot.SetGlowForChest(currentContainer);
			}
		}
		NewCraftingUI.Close(quiet: true);
		Main.playerInventory = true;
		UILinkPointNavigator.ForceMovementCooldown(PlayerInput.CurrentProfile.InventoryMoveCD);
		if (PlayerInput.GrappleAndInteractAreShared)
		{
			PlayerInput.Triggers.JustPressed.Grapple = false;
		}
		Main.PipsUseGrid = false;
		chestX = x;
		chestY = y;
	}

	public void CloseSign(bool quiet = false)
	{
		if (sign > -1)
		{
			sign = -1;
			Main.editSign = false;
			Main.npcChatText = string.Empty;
			if (!quiet)
			{
				SoundEngine.PlaySound(11);
			}
		}
	}

	public void OpenSign(int s)
	{
		IngameUIWindows.CloseAll(quiet: true);
		if (s >= 0)
		{
			SoundEngine.PlaySound(10);
			sign = s;
			Main.npcChatText = Main.sign[s].text;
		}
	}

	private void LaunchMinecartHook(int myX, int myY)
	{
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2((float)Main.mouseX + Main.screenPosition.X, (float)Main.mouseY + Main.screenPosition.Y);
		vector = new Vector2(myX * 16 + 8, myY * 16 + 8);
		RemoveAllGrapplingHooks();
		Projectile.NewProjectile(GetProjectileSource_TileInteraction(myX, myY), vector.X, vector.Y, 0f, 0f, 403, 0, 0f, whoAmI);
		releaseHook = false;
	}

  /** NPC.HasSpecialEventText（NPC.cs:96081-96105 1:1）：五环境态按原版顺序各 1/3
   *  掷骰，命中且语言包有 "<Cat>SpecialText.<Event>" 池 → 随机一条。
   *  墓园（ZoneGraveyard）→ 派对（PartyIsUp；HasNoPartyText 表未移植——本表 NPC
   *  全部有派对文案）→ 雨（raining 且非风暴）→ 风日（IsItAHappyWindyDay）→ 风暴 */
  private npcSpecialEventText(npc: TownNPC): string | null {
    const cat = Game.SPECIAL_TEXT_CATEGORY[npc.vanillaId];
    if (!cat) return null;
    const wd = this.world.weather;
    const pick = (suffix: string): string | null =>
      (Math.random() < 1 / 3 ? Lang.specialText(`${cat}SpecialText.${suffix}`) : null);
    if (this.scene.zoneGraveyard) { const t = pick('Graveyard'); if (t) return t; }
    if (partyIsUp(this.partyState)) { const t = pick('Party'); if (t) return t; }
    if (wd.raining && !wd.shouldUseStormMusic) { const t = pick('Rain'); if (t) return t; }
    if (wd.shouldUseWindyDayMusic) { const t = pick('Windy'); if (t) return t; }
    if (wd.shouldUseStormMusic) { const t = pick('Storm'); if (t) return t; }
    return null;
  }

  /** 开宝箱统一入口:锁定箱(原版 Chest.locked)需金钥匙,首次开启消耗 */
  private tryOpenChest(chest: { locked?: boolean }): void {
    // 物品保护（v3 房间制）：非房主禁开宝箱（v1 无箱子内容同步，取放全在本地——门禁开箱即拦截）
    if (!this.netCanChestInteract()) { this.netDeny(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.RoomProtectItems')); return; }
    if (chest.locked) {
      const keyId = ITEM_BY_KEY['vi_327_golden_key'];
      let keySlot = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < this.player.inv.slots.length; i++) {
        const sl = this.player.inv.slots[i];
        if (sl && sl.id === keyId && sl.stack > 0) { keySlot = i; break; }
      }
      if (keySlot < 0) { this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.ChestLocked')); this.input.rightDown = false; return; }
      this.player.inv.removeAt(keySlot, 1);
      chest.locked = false;
      this.sfx.play('tink');
      // 成就：DUNGEON_HEIST 的 prog(19)（Chest.cs:346 Unlock——金钥匙开锁金箱）。
      // 原版另有暗影箱 event 20（Chest.cs:363/384/458 → BIG_BOOTY）；本仓锁定箱
      // 模型统一走金钥匙（无暗影钥匙区分），event 20 暂不接线（偏差已记录）
      this.achievements.notifyProgressionEvent(19);
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.ChestUnlocked'));
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    }
    // 陷阱箱 441/468(Player.cs:21303 语义):开启即触发电路 + 向玩家八方射镖
    const cxy = chest as { x?: number; y?: number };
    const csh = cxy.x !== undefined && cxy.y !== undefined ? this.wiring?.sheetOf(cxy.x, cxy.y) ?? -1 : -1;
    if ((csh === 441 || csh === 468) && cxy.x !== undefined && cxy.y !== undefined) {
      this.wiring?.hitSwitch(cxy.x, cxy.y);
      const cxp = cxy.x * TILE + 16, cyp = cxy.y * TILE + 16;
      for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
        const ang = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
        this.entities.add(new TrapShot(cxp, cyp, Math.cos(ang) * 6, Math.sin(ang) * 6, 20, 'dart'), 'projectiles');
      }
      this.sfx.play('tink');
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.ChestTrapped'));
    }
    this.openChest?.(chest as never);

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T02:00:05.797Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Default maxTrackedInstances = 0 (no cap) unless explicitly set. So the cap mechanism exists but defaults to uncapped. The main anti-spam mechanism in vanilla is:
1. Per-type instance replacement in LegacySoundPlayer (tracked sounds — dig, tink, splash, etc. reuse/stop instances).
2. `DoesSoundScaleWithAmbientVolume` + soundVolume=0 gate.
3. The distance attenuation (2500px) — which the impl has.

And critically: vanilla `PlaySound(0, x, y)` for dig is a **tracked** sound — LegacySoundPlayer case 0 picks `num13 = Main.rand.Next(3)` and **stops the previous instance of that variant** before playing. So at most 3 concurrent dig sounds. Similarly other tracked types. That's the anti-spam.

Implementation: every `play()` creates a brand-new AudioBufferSourceNode, never stops anything → unbounded overlap. Combined with per-hit dig (which vanilla has), this would be spammy. But since impl only plays dig on break, it's less spammy. Still, no cap.

OK. I'm confident in my findings. Let me check if agent 2 finished... I'll write the report now and incorporate if it arrives. Actually, let me give it one more moment by doing a final verification of the DD2 trigger claim and the impl's MinionProj lines (already verified at 747/773/852).

One more: I claimed food plays 'drink' (Item3) but vanilla default food = Item2. Let me verify which impl sites are food. Let me check site 3709 and 4261-4368.
```

export function hitCritters(self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, game: GameHooks): boolean {
  const list = (game as unknown as { critters?: () => unknown[] }).critters?.() ?? [];
  for (const ent of list) {
    const c = ent as CritterLike;
    if (c.dead) continue;
    if (projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, c)) {
      c.hurt(game);
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** 弹幕命中怪物:播受击音效(原版 NPC.StrikeNPC 语义——任意伤害来源都响,
 *  近战挥砍早已如此;弹幕此前漏了) */
export function playEnemyHitSound(game: GameHooks, e: { def?: { hitSound?: string[] } }): void {
  const files = e.def?.hitSound;
  if (files && files.length) game.playSfxFiles(files, 0.9);
}

/** 敌对弹幕命中玩家(原版 Projectile.Damage_EVP,Projectile.cs:13706-13830):

### 覆盖清单(8 类 × 项)

| 类 | 已接 ✅ | 条件偏差 ⚠️ / 近似 🔀 | 缺失 ❌ |
|---|---|---|---|

| 2 死亡族 | 玩家死亡(pkilled) | 敌怪死亡(Boss 被泛化成 roar) | — |
| 3 战斗动作 | 弓/枪三族/鞭/召唤杖/投掷/手雷/喝药/摔落接触伤害 | 生命魔力水晶(Item4 错接 Item3)、食物(Item2 咀嚼错接 Item3 吞咽) | — |
| 4 交互族 | 开关门、放块、拾取(coin/pickup 分档)、商店/收税 | 合成(缺 MenuTick 点击声) | **开箱/关箱**、锁定箱 Unlock(22)、**挖掘逐击音**、双跳(16) |
| 5 事件族 | Boss 召唤 Roar(编号+蜂后例外+首播门)、血月开始(原版本就无声) | — | — |
| 6 环境族 | 雷声(近似,已审)、雨声、BGM 交叉淡入 | — | **瀑布/岩浆瀑环境循环(34/35)**、暴雪 Ambient loop |
| 7 UI 族 | 按钮/切栏/聊天/成就面板(MenuTick)、成就解锁(原版无声,一致) | — | **最优重铸专属音(best_reforge)**、非最优档编号(Item37≠Tink 21) |
| 8 播放条件 | 2500px 衰减、x=-1 不衰减(UI 声)、Boss Roar 首播门 | — | **同型实例替换与并发上限**(叠加爆音风险) |

另:资产层三处死链(详见 #1/#30/#3):`Custom/` 前缀 12 文件、`Item_52/53/56` 三文件、`Item_37/Item_2/Item_4` 未提取;`Unlock.wav` 已入库却零引用。

### 最需修复 TOP 7

1. **`Sfx.ts:63-66` DD2 四音效路径死链**——`Custom/` 前缀指向不存在的目录,且合成 switch 无兜底分支 → 塔射击/爆炸/电击永久静音。修复=删前缀(文件就在顶层),4 行,零风险。
2. **挖掘逐击音缺失(`Game.ts:5438-5440`)**——原版每击响(`WorldGen.cs:66483` 经 `KillTile(fail:true)`),且按原版 tile 表分 Grass(6)/Tink(21)/Dig(0);现为"只破坏完成响 + 内部 key 正则分档"。这是"挖矿没有节奏声"的直接根因。
3. **最优重铸专属音(`Game.ts:11294`)**——best-tier 应播 `SoundID.BestReforge`(`SoundID.cs:1097`)+彩虹粒子+60t 冷却(`Main.cs:41347-41383`),非 best 应播 Item37;现恒播 tink(21)。需提取 `best_reforge`/`Item_37` 资产并接 `rollPrefix` 的 topTier 返回值。
4. **开箱/关箱无音(`UI.ts:1951/1967`)**——原版 MenuOpen(10)/MenuClose(11)(`Player.cs:32462-32464`);顺带把锁定箱开锁从 tink 换成已在库的 `Unlock.wav`(22)。
5. **Boss 死亡 roar 泛化(`Enemy.ts:4912`)**——原版只播该 NPC 自己的 DeathSound(`NPC.cs:78975-78983`);数据侧 `killedSound` 已具备,把 killBossFlag 分支改为"优先 killedSound,空才 roar"即可。
6. **并发上限缺失(`Sfx.ts:190-219`)**——无同型实例替换(原版 `LegacySoundPlayer.cs:444-447`)、无 MaxTrackedInstances(原版 `SoundEngine.cs:130`);世界生成/塌落场景有叠爆音风险,与既有记忆条目同源。
7. **玩家受伤 voice 分档(`Sfx.ts:40` + `Player.ts` 四处)**——style 应恒 1 + voicePitchOffset,voiceVariant 2/3(FemaleHit/DefaultPlayerHurt)与 stoned/狼人/冰霜/骨套变体未接;建议把发声收进 `Player.damage()` 统一出口,消除各调用点手播的漏播面。

**审计中已消解一项**:审计开局曾捕获 `Game.ts:2607/4687` 传非法名 `'swing'`(tsc TS2345 ×2,且 WAV_MAP/合成 switch 均无此键 → 运行时无声);审计中途被并行会话修掉,现已无此错误。此模式值得警惕——`play()` 的字面量参数无运行时校验,拼错即静默失效,建议后续加断言或把 SfxName 收窄为 const 对象键。

## 5. 切物品栏 — 无音效

原版**切热栏不播任何音**。滚轮/Hotbar±:`Player.HandleHotbarControls`(`Terraria/Player.cs:31282-31312`)只 `selectedItemState.Select(...)`,无 PlaySound;数字键 1-0:`Terraria/Player.cs:24798-24837` → `selectedItemState.Select(num6)`(`:24879`),同样无声。另外 SoundID 里**不存在 ItemGrab**(最接近的是 `Grab = 7`,`Terraria.ID/SoundID.cs:35`)。

## 6. 拾取物品 — Grab(7) vs CoinPickup(38)

统一表达式:`SoundEngine.PlaySound(newItem.IsACoin ? 38 : 7)`,即**钱币走 38(CoinPickup),其余走 7(Grab)**,style 1、无位置参数(满音量):

| 场景 | 证据 |
|---|---|
| 叠进已有格 | `Terraria/Player.cs:38828`(`GetItem_FillIntoOccupiedSlot`) |
| 叠进虚空袋已有格 | `Terraria/Player.cs:38781` |
| 放入空格 | `Terraria/Player.cs:38896`(`GetItem_FillEmptyInventorySlot`) |
| 放入虚空袋空格 | `Terraria/Player.cs:38871` |
| 弹药槽填弹 | 恒为 Grab(7) | `Terraria/Player.cs:38601,38648` |
| 星云强化/红心/魔力星等弹体拾取 | Grab(7) | `Terraria/Player.cs:34598,34612,34618,34632` |

**Coins(18) 不用于拾取**;它的用途是:卖给商店(`Terraria.UI/ItemSlot.cs:788,1117`)和钱币堆方块放置/破坏(`Terraria/WorldGen.cs:60301,66594`)。编号:`SoundID.cs:35`(Grab=7)、`:57`(Coins=18)、`:97`(CoinPickup=38)。

## 8. 成就解锁

`SoundID.AchievementComplete`(trackable,名字 "achievement_complete",`CreateTrackable` → `LegacySoundStyle(42, idx, 1, Sound)`,**无数字编号**,type 42=Trackable)。播放:`AchievementInitializer.OnAchievementCompleted` 先 `FindActiveSound` 去重(同一时间只播一个)再 `PlayTrackedSound`:

- `Terraria.Initializers/AchievementInitializer.cs:732`(去重)、`:734`(播放)、`:728`(方法头,`:723` 订阅)
- 定义:`Terraria.ID/SoundID.cs:817`;trackable 机制:`Terraria.ID/SoundID.cs:1185-1203`(`return new LegacySoundStyle(42, count, ...)`,`SoundID.cs:105` Trackable=42)

## 9. 雨声 / 风声 / 雷 / 瀑布

| 项 | 结论 | 证据 |
|---|---|---|
| **雨声混音** | 它是**音乐轨 28**(不是 PlaySound)。`Main.UpdateAudio` 每帧:`num11 = cloudAlpha/9*10*0.5 + 0.5`,乘 `(1-shimmerAlpha)`,clamp≤1,再乘 `atmo^4`;条件 `SceneMetrics.ZoneRain &amp;&amp; !SceneMetrics.ZoneSnow` 时 `UpdateAmbientCueState(..., ambientVolume*num11)`,否则以 0.005/帧淡出 | `Terraria/Main.cs:11467-11491`(vol 公式 `:11470`,条件 `:11480`,淡入/淡出 `:11484/:11488`);淡入/淡出实现 `Terraria.Audio/LegacyAudioSystem.cs:231`(+0.005)、`:252`(-stoppingSpeed) |
| **风声(有)** | **音乐轨 45**。`num7 = |windSpeedCurrent|*0.7 + 0.3`,×(1-shimmerAlpha),clamp,×0.9,×`atmo^4`;条件 `|windSpeedCurrent| &gt;= 20/50 (=0.4) &amp;&amp; SceneMetrics.SurfaceAtmospherics` | `Terraria/Main.cs:11493-11519`(vol `:11496`,`×0.9` 在 `:11505`,阈值 `:11508`) |
| **Thunder(43)** | `Main.UpdateWeather` 中 `thunderDelay` 减到 0 时,位置 = SceneMetrics.Center.X ± thunderDistance*15、Y 压到 worldSurface*16-500 以上;仅当 `FocusHelper.UpdateBackgroundThunder &amp;&amp; !thunderSkipSound`。播放层随机 6 个 Thunder_0..5、pitch ±0.1 | 播放 `Terraria/Main.cs:58148`(方法 `:58124`,延迟生成 `:58101-58109`);变体与音高 `Terraria.Audio/LegacySoundPlayer.cs:459-477`,资源 `:182-187`;编号 `SoundID.cs:107` |
| **Waterfall(34) / Lavafall(35)** | `Main.Ambience()` 每 15 帧刷一次循环声:有强度时 `PlaySound(34, x, y, (int)strength)`(style 参数=强度,直接当音量系数用),无则 `PlaySound(34, camX, camY, 0)` 停止。播放层:`vol *= strength/50(clamp 1)`,瀑布再 `×0.2`、熔岩 `×0.65`,均 `×(1-shimmerAlpha)`,瀑布 pitch=-0.2 | 触发 `Terraria/Main.cs:13417,13424`(34)、`:13440,13446`(35)(方法 `Ambience` `:13407`);混音 `Terraria.Audio/LegacySoundPlayer.cs:1243-1251`(34)、`:1280-1288`(35);编号 `SoundID.cs:89`(34)/`:91`(35) |

## 10. BGM 交叉淡入淡出(LegacyAudioSystem)

- 每帧遍历 1..maxMusic-1:`Terraria/Main.cs:11448-11530`;`isMainTrackAudible = musicFade[curMusic] &gt; 0.25f`(`:11448`);当前曲 `UpdateCommonTrack`(`:11524`),其他曲 `UpdateCommonTrackTowardStopping`(`:11528`)。
- 淡入:`tempFade += 0.005f/帧`(上限 1),未播放则 `Reuse()+Play()+SetVariable("Volume", totalVolume)` —— `Terraria.Audio/LegacyAudioSystem.cs:281-307`(`+0.005` 在 `:285`)。
- 淡出:仅当 `isMainTrackAudible` 时 `tempFade -= 0.005f/帧`;若 `Main.curMusic == 0` 则直接归零;归零后 `Stop(AudioStopOptions.Immediate)` —— `Terraria.Audio/LegacyAudioSystem.cs:309-341`(`:316-323` 即 0.005 与立即停)。

## 11. 血月开始 — 没有专属音效

**Tall gate finding**: `Door.ts:141` calls `sfx?.('door')` — and **`'door'` is NOT a valid SfxName**! The valid names are `door_open` and `door_close`. So tall gates:
- pass an invalid name → `playWav('door')` → `WAV_MAP['door']` is undefined → returns false → falls to synth switch → no `'door'` case → **silently does nothing**.
- Worse, this is `this.sfx.play(n as SfxName)` with an `as` cast at Game.ts:11060, so TypeScript cannot catch it (unlike the `'swing'` case which was a literal and got flagged).

Also note: vanilla tall gate plays **always DoorOpen(8)** regardless of direction (`WorldGen.cs:51786`); here `shiftTallGate` receives `closing` but plays one sound regardless — if they'd used a valid name, either `door_open` or `door_close` would deviate from vanilla's always-8. Double bug: invalid name + direction semantics.

This is a genuinely new finding (my earlier grep at old-line 10807/10818 showed `play(n as SfxName)` but I didn't resolve `n`).

**审计中已消解一项**:`Game.ts` 早期版本在 2607/4687 行传非法字面量 `'swing'`(tsc TS2345 ×2,且 WAV_MAP/合成 switch 均无此键 → 运行时无声);审计中途被并行会话修掉。但 #15 高门的 `'door'` 死名经 `as SfxName` 断言**逃过 tsc**,是同模式第二例——坐实了 `play()` 需要运行时校验(非法名 console.warn 或断言),否则拼错即静默失效。

### 覆盖清单(8 类 × 项)

| 类 | 已接 ✅ | 条件偏差 ⚠️ / 近似 🔀 | 缺失 ❌ |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 受伤族 | 敌怪被击(hitSound 表+近战回退)、Boss 被击无放大(与原版一致) | 玩家受伤(style 应恒 1+pitch;女声/voiceOverride/stoned/狼人/冰霜/骨套变体全缺;靠调用点手播有漏播面) | — |
| 2 死亡族 | 玩家死亡(pkilled) | 敌怪死亡(Boss 被泛化成 roar) | — |
| 3 战斗动作 | 弓/枪三族/鞭/召唤杖/投掷/手雷/喝药 | 生命魔力水晶(Item4 错接 Item3)、食物(Item2 咀嚼错接 Item3) | MaxMana(25) 效果音 |
| 4 交互族 | 开关门、放块(逻辑)、拾取分档方向、商店/收税、热栏切换(原版本无声) | 拾取 38/18 混名、铺轨 Item52 资产缺失 | **开箱/关箱**、锁定箱 Unlock(22)、**挖掘逐击音**、**双跳(16)**、**高门开关死名** |
| 5 事件族 | Boss 召唤 Roar(编号+蜂后例外+首播门)、血月开始(原版本就无声) | — | — |
| 6 环境族 | 雷声(近似,已审)、BGM 交叉淡入 | 雨声(公式四偏差) | **风声轨 45(差一步接线)**、**瀑布/岩浆瀑环境循环(34/35)** |
| 7 UI 族 | 槽位按钮/聊天/成就面板(MenuTick)、craft 动作 Grab(与原版一致) | — | **成就解锁音+去重**、配方选中 MenuTick、**最优重铸专属音(best_reforge)**、非最优档编号(Item37≠Tink 21) |

资产层死链汇总:`Custom/` 前缀 12 文件(#1)、`Item_52/53/56` 三文件(#30)、`Item_37/Item_2/Item_4/best_reforge` 未提取(#3/#11/#12);`Unlock.wav` 已入库却零引用(#5)。

### 最需修复 TOP 8(补遗后重排)

1. **`Sfx.ts:63-66` DD2 四音效路径死链**——`Custom/` 前缀指向不存在的目录,且合成 switch 无兜底分支 → 塔射击/爆炸/电击永久静音。修复=删前缀(文件就在顶层),4 行,零风险。
2. **挖掘逐击音缺失(`Game.ts:5438-5440`)**——原版每击响(`WorldGen.cs:66483` 经 `KillTile(fail:true)`),且按原版 tile 表分 Grass(6)/Tink(21)/Dig(0)(玻璃 Shatter、雪 Item48/49 等特例同源);现为"只破坏完成响 + 内部 key 正则分档"。这是"挖矿没有节奏声"的直接根因。
3. **最优重铸专属音(`Game.ts:11294`)**——best-tier 应播 `SoundID.BestReforge`(`SoundID.cs:1097`)+彩虹粒子+60t 冷却(`Main.cs:41347-41383`),非 best 应播 Item37;现恒播 tink(21)。需提取 `best_reforge`/`Item_37` 资产并接 `rollPrefix` 的 topTier 返回值。
4. **开箱/关箱无音(`UI.ts:1951/1967`)**——原版 MenuOpen(10)/MenuClose(11)/容器切换 MenuTick(12)(`Player.cs:32459-32464`);顺带把锁定箱开锁从 tink 换成已在库的 `Unlock.wav`(22)。
5. **高门 `'door'` 死名(`Door.ts:141` + `Game.ts:11060`)**——非法 SfxName 经 `as` 断言逃过 tsc → 高门开关永久静音;且原版高门恒播 8 不分开关(`WorldGen.cs:51786`)。修一行;同时建议给 `Sfx.play()` 加运行时校验根治此类(与已消解的 `'swing'` 同模式)。
6. **Boss 死亡 roar 泛化(`Enemy.ts:4912`)**——原版只播该 NPC 自己的 DeathSound(`NPC.cs:78975-78983`);数据侧 `killedSound` 已具备,把 killBossFlag 分支改为"优先 killedSound,空才 roar"即可。
7. **并发上限 + 成就音合并修复(`Sfx.ts:190-219` + `Game.ts:1799`)**——无同型实例替换(原版 `LegacySoundPlayer.cs:443-454`)、无 MaxTrackedInstances(`SoundEngine.cs:130`);在 `Sfx` 上加 per-name 活跃实例计数,可同时覆盖叠爆音与成就解锁音的"同一时刻只播一个"去重(`AchievementInitializer.cs:732`),一处改动两个问题。
8. **玩家受伤 voice 分档(`Sfx.ts:40` + `Player.ts` 四处)**——style 应恒 1 + voicePitchOffset(`Player.cs:38087-38102`),voiceVariant 2/3(FemaleHit/DefaultPlayerHurt)与 stoned/狼人/冰霜/骨套变体未接;建议把发声收进 `Player.damage()` 统一出口,消除各调用点手播的漏播面。

次优先(资产管线,逻辑已 1:1):矿车三音 `Item_52/53/56.wav` 提取(#30)、风声轨 45 接线(枚举+资产已备,#27)、雨声公式对齐四偏差(#25)、拾取 38/18 拆名(#17)。
```
